- **shugui-perspective**: 叔贵（叔贵健身创始人，《健身笔记》作者）的循证科普/居家训练/体态矫正思维框架与表达方式。基于约30+条来源（一手约45-50%）深度调研， 提炼5个核心心智模型（生活服务金字塔、整体齿轮联动代偿、降阶-标准-升阶适配、热量缺口优先、反盲从高强度跟练等）、10条决策启发式和完整的表达DNA（第三人称自称+啾咪、先结论再拆解、数据化清单）。 用途：作为居家训练/体态矫正/减脂科普的思维顾问，用叔贵的视角判断训练是否服务生活、识别智商税、设计难度分层。 当用户提到「用叔贵的视角」「叔贵会怎么看」「叔贵模式」「叔贵思维」时使用。即使用户只是说「帮我用叔贵的角度想想」也应触发。 不在一般性健身闲聊中自动触发，需明确指向该人物视角。 ([superj0107/shugui-perspective](https://github.com/superj0107/shugui-perspective/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))

- **shui-on-xintiandi**: 瑞安房地产新天地系列方法论蒸馏。涵盖罗康瑞城市更新理念、开发性保护策略、历史建筑改造、长期持有运营、商业+住宅+办公综合开发、多城市复制逻辑、品牌升维战略、轻资产策略。 触发词：「新天地」「瑞安」「罗康瑞」「Shui On」「城市更新」「石库门」「蟠龙天地」「岭南天地」「武汉天地」「开发性保护」「东台里」「社区品牌」。 当用户分析城市更新项目、历史街区改造、综合开发、存量商业改造时激活。 ([ykjshenzhen2008-ship-it/commercial-real-estate-skills](https://github.com/ykjshenzhen2008-ship-it/commercial-real-estate-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/shui-on-xintiandi))

- **shuip-components**: Use when choosing which shuip item fits a need, or composing shuip components and blocks — provides the full item catalog, install/import conventions, and composition notes. Use before building UI that shuip may already provide. ([plvo/shuip](https://github.com/plvo/shuip/tree/HEAD/packages/registry/skills/shuip-components) · ★12)
- **shuip-forms**: Use when building or editing a form in a project that uses shuip — covers shuip's react-hook-form (rhf-) and tanstack-form (tsf-) field components, their install commands, validation, submission, and accessible error/loading state. Use before hand-writing shadcn FormField boilerplate. ([plvo/shuip](https://github.com/plvo/shuip/tree/HEAD/packages/registry/skills/shuip-forms) · ★12)
- **shuip-overview**: Use when working in a project that uses shuip and you need to know what shuip provides, how to install items from its registry, or which shuip skill/category to reach for. Triggers on shuip imports (@/components/ui/shuip/...) or a shuip registry reference (@shuip/&lt;name&gt;). - **shukatsu-distill**: 日本就活（金融・商社・コンサル・IT・エンジニア職対応）の知識蒸留・素材化ツール。内定者経験談を素材として取り込み、成功パターンを蒸留し、逆面接で本人の経験を素材化する（es-coach / mensetsu-report の知識ベースを作る上流）。/shukatsu-distill ingest, /shukatsu-distill distill, /shukatsu-distill grill（逆面接で本人の経験を素材化）, /shukatsu-distill review（自動収集候補の複核）, /shukatsu-distill log（本人の選考結果の記録）の5モードで動作。ES添削は /es-coach、面接対策レポートは /mensetsu-report。 ([jiayi-ThE-CREATOR/es-coach-skills](https://github.com/jiayi-ThE-CREATOR/es-coach-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/shukatsu-distill))
- **shukatsu-radar**: Job-hunting copilot for students and new grads looking for jobs in Japan (就活, 新卒, インターン). Finds open internships and 本選考 openings from public sources such as 魔法のスプレッドシート, 外資就活, and LabBase, filtered by the user's graduation year and target roles. It also tracks deadlines, researches companies, maintains an ES story bank and drafts ES (エントリーシート), and tracks applications. Use when the user asks things like "find internships", "what's open now", "冬インターン", "本選考", "any deadlines coming up", "research company X", "write/polish my ES", "add company X / update status", or "daily search". Also use it… ([ushi02/shukatsu-radar](https://github.com/ushi02/shukatsu-radar/tree/HEAD/skills/shukatsu-radar))

- **si-bian**: 把模糊想法一个词一个词推到有边界。用户有一个说不清的想法、理论或问题，想搞清楚本质、找到边界、检查前人理论支持时使用。不直接给答案，不做文案写作，不做内容检测。 ([Dyno-en/si-bian](https://github.com/Dyno-en/si-bian/tree/HEAD/skills/si-bian))
- **si-blame-the-task**: The always-on governor for the pack. When self-assessment is hard — am I right, is this done, do I know this — suspect the information environment, not your capability. Don't introspect harder; ENRICH it (run it, read the real file, check docs, ask) and mine data from the past and data from others. Reach a verdict, but hold it loosely and name what you didn't verify. Applies silently to any claim about your own correctness, completeness, or knowledge; dispatches the sibling si-* skills. ([jpoindexter/self-insight-agent-skills](https://github.com/jpoindexter/self-insight-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/si-blame-the-task))
- **si-calibration-gate**: Confidence is not accuracy. Before asserting a fact or marking work "done," separate how sure you feel from how likely you are right, attach explicit uncertainty, and apply a correction factor. Peak certainty is exactly where error hides. Use before any factual claim, "this is correct/complete/fixed," or confidence estimate. ([jpoindexter/self-insight-agent-skills](https://github.com/jpoindexter/self-insight-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/si-calibration-gate))

- **si-competence-floor**: In domains where you are weak, you are least able to detect that you are weak — the skills needed to do the task are the same skills needed to judge it. In unfamiliar territory, distrust the "looks right" signal and verify externally. Also corrects the expert's mirror error: assuming the user knows what you know. Use on unfamiliar stacks, languages, or domains. ([jpoindexter/self-insight-agent-skills](https://github.com/jpoindexter/self-insight-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/si-competence-floor))
- **si-confidence-build**: Provide evidence-based exercises to build confidence. ([ichigo137/J-A-R-V-I-S](https://github.com/ichigo137/J-A-R-V-I-S/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/si-confidence-build))
- **si-confidence-source**: Audit WHY something feels right before trusting the feeling. Three false cues — fluency (easy to generate), pseudorelevance (knowing a lot around the topic), and inherited self-view ("I'm good at this") — produce confidence unconnected to accuracy. Name the cue; if it's one of these, downgrade and verify. Use whenever a confident answer arrives without an explicit check. ([jpoindexter/self-insight-agent-skills](https://github.com/jpoindexter/self-insight-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/si-confidence-source))
- **si-construe-the-situation**: Most prediction errors are misread situations, not unknown selves. Before claiming "this will work," get the actual situation — read the real file, check the real env/config/versions, look at the real data shape and the error paths — instead of predicting against a clean, abstract, happy-path sketch. Lower confidence in proportion to how much of the real situation you haven't seen. Use before predicting any runtime behavior, claiming code will work, or estimating an outcome. ([jpoindexter/self-insight-agent-skills](https://github.com/jpoindexter/self-insight-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/si-construe-the-situation))

- **si-corrective-feedback**: Feedback is the riskiest thing you do — nearly 40% of feedback interventions make performance WORSE. - **si-define-the-criterion**: Vague success words — done, clean, secure, fast, good, robust — get defined self-servingly to flatter whatever you produced. Use on relative judgments, uniqueness claims, and estimates of how much you control an outcome. ([sh5623/self-improvement](https://github.com/sh5623/self-improvement/tree/HEAD/plugins/self-improvement/skills/si-improve) · ★1)
- **si-improve**: Use when a convention, document, or procedure was missing, wrong, or ambiguous and it cost you time during the work, and that gap looks likely to recur in other files, features, or tasks. Also use when the end-of-work check ("of what bit me, what will recur?") surfaces a gap, and on requests like "self-improvement", "fix the convention", "we have a convention gap". Do NOT load for: one-off mistakes, fixing the code bug itself, trimming bloated convention docs (si-archive owns that), or first-time setup in a project without the system (si-init owns that). ([sh5623/self-improvement](https://github.com/sh5623/self-improvement/tree/HEAD/skills/si-improve) · ★1)
- **si-init**: Bootstrap or register a project's convention repair system in Codex, preserving existing records and separating Codex instruction wiring from Claude. Use for initial setup or checking the setup after a plugin update. ([sh5623/self-improvement](https://github.com/sh5623/self-improvement/tree/HEAD/plugins/self-improvement/skills/si-init) · ★1)
- **si-init**: Use to bootstrap a project once, when it has no self-improvement data file (convention changelog and routing table). Triggers include "set up self-improvement", "si-init", and si-improve / si-archive / convention-smith failing to find the data file. Also re-run it right after a plugin update — it compares the data file's version stamp and repairs stale template wording (project data is never touched). Re-running is safe and idempotent: when it detects an existing system it registers and repairs rather than overwriting. ([sh5623/self-improvement](https://github.com/sh5623/self-improvement/tree/HEAD/skills/si-init) · ★1)

- **sicherheits-check**: Prüft einen Linux-Server (VPS) nur lesend auf Sicherheitsprobleme - von außen offene Ports (auch die, die Docker an der Firewall vorbei öffnet), SSH-Einstellungen, veraltete Software in Containern (PHP, Node, n8n, Traefik, LiteLLM), Abstürze in Logs, offen lesbare Secrets - und gleicht die gefundenen Versionen mit aktuellen Sicherheitslücken ab. Nutzen, wenn jemand seinen Server prüfen oder absichern will, nach Meldungen wie PHP2Shell, oder als regelmäßiger Check. Der Check ändert nie etwas; Fixes führt der Nutzer aus oder gibt sie einzeln frei, nach Snapshot. - **sicherheits-check**: Prüft ein Projekt auf die fünf Fehlermuster KI-gestützter Entwicklung — Zugangsdaten im Code, erfundene Abhängigkeiten, unsichere Voreinstellungen, ungeprüfte Auslieferung, fehlende Sicherheits-Header. Führt je Projekt eine Historie über gefundene und behobene Befunde. Trigger; "sicherheitscheck", "/sicherheits-check", "prüf die sicherheit", "security check", "ist das sicher". Läuft außerdem automatisch bei JEDER weitermachen.md und beim Anlegen eines neuen Projekts. Sicherheit hat Vorrang vor normalen Aufgaben. - **sichuan-dialect-coach**: 四川话速成助手 v1.0.0（知识库型）。面向普通话使用者的四川话「方言开口教练」：单句句卡、火锅/串串/茶馆/问路/购物等真实场景、语气词系统、叠词儿化、称呼艺术、夸人/吐槽语境、俗语歇后语、风险分级与地域差异提示。不是翻译器、不支持语音识别。四川话属西南官话片区，大量说法与重庆、云南、贵州相通。触发词：四川话、四川方言、重庆话、西南官话、川渝、巴适、安逸、要得、啥子、啷个、咋个、瓜娃子、摆龙门阵、抵拢倒拐、耙耳朵、雄起、教我说、怎么说、速成。 ([bashanbro/sichuan-dialect-skill](https://github.com/bashanbro/sichuan-dialect-skill/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))

- **sichuan-mandarin**: Transform text into Sichuan Mandarin （四川話 / 川渝話） — a Southwestern Mandarin variety of the 成渝片 zone covering Sichuan and Chongqing, marked by light 「兒」化，莫/莫得 negation, abundant sentence-final particles （嘛/噻/嘞/哈/撒）, signature lexicon （巴適/安逸/莫得/搞快點/龜兒/撇脫/擺龍門陣/牙尖/假打/鬼火冒）, blunt 江湖 humor, and a self-mocking 川劇丑角 voice. Three intensity levels from light Sichuan flavor on a Putonghua backbone to heavy 張麻子-style 江湖 cadence. Useful for creative writing, cultural appreciation, language education, or humor.
